\displaystyle\leq\sup_{q\in\left[-2\lambda,2\lambda\right]}\bigg|P\left\{\frac{a(\mathsf{R}_{g^{\star},k},D)-a(\mathsf{R}_{g^{\star},k}^{\prime},D)}{\sqrt{2v_{g^{\star},k}\left(D\right)}}\leq z_{1-\beta/2}+q\right\}-\left(1-\beta/2\right)\bigg|

\displaystyle+\left(1-P\left\{\mathcal{W}_{\lambda}\left(D\right)\mid D\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{z\in\mathbb{R}}\bigg|P\left\{\frac{a(\mathsf{R}_{g^{\star},k},D)-a(\mathsf{R}_{g^{\star},k}^{\prime},D)}{\sqrt{2v_{g^{\star},k}\left(D\right)}}\leq z\mid D\right\}-\Phi(z)\bigg|+2\lambda+\left(1-P\left\{\mathcal{W}_{\lambda}\left(D\right)\mid D\right\}\right),

as required.

E.7.
Proof of
Lemma

D.7

E.7.1.
Part (i)

Observe that we can write

a(\mathsf{R}_{g^{\star},k},D)-a(\mathsf{R}_{g^{\star},k}^{\prime},D)=\frac{1}{g^{\star}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{g}\left(\overline{a}(\mathsf{r}_{\ell},D)-\overline{a}(\mathsf{r}_{\ell}^{\prime},D)\right),

where
\overline{a}\left(\cdot,D\right)
is defined in (
D.33
). We have that

(Hölder)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[|\overline{a}(\mathsf{r}_{\ell},D)-\overline{a}(\mathsf{r}_{\ell}^{\prime},D)|^{3}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\overline{a}(\mathsf{r}_{\ell},D)-\overline{a}(\mathsf{r}_{\ell}^{\prime},D)\right)^{4}\right]\right)^{3/4}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{6}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\overline{a}(\mathsf{r}_{\ell},D)\right)^{4}\right]\right)^{3/4}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{6}3^{2}(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{3}(\Gamma^{(2)}_{k,\varphi,b})^{3/4}\penalty\ ,

where the second inequality follows from Hölder’s inequality, the
binomial theorem, and the fact that
\mathsf{r}_{\ell}
and
\mathsf{r}_{\ell}^{\prime}

are exchangeable. The final inequality follows from
Theorem

D.2
. Consequently,
we find that

(Markov)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[|\overline{a}(\mathsf{r}_{\ell},D)-\overline{a}(\mathsf{r}_{\ell}^{\prime},D)|^{3}\mid D\right]}{\left(g^{\star}\right)^{1/2}\left(2v_{1,k}\left(D\right)\right)^{3/2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{3}(\Gamma^{(2)}_{k,\varphi,b})^{3/4}}{\delta\left(g^{\star}\right)^{1/2}\left(v_{1,k}\left(D\right)\right)^{3/2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\xi}{z_{1-\beta/2}}\frac{(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{3}(\Gamma^{(2)}_{k,\varphi,b})^{3/4}}{\delta\left(v_{1,k}\left(D\right)\right)^{2}}\penalty\ ,

holds with probability
1-\delta
. The Lemma then follows by the Berry-Esseen inequality.

E.7.2.
Part (ii)

Our bound is based on the following two conditional concentration
inequalities. Both arguments are based on a Chernoff-type maximal
inequality, due to
Steiger, 1970
.

Lemma E.5.

Suppose that
4.1
and
4.2
hold,
that the data
D
are independent and identically distributed,
and that the eighth-order split
\zeta^{8}
stability is finite.

(i)
For all
t>0
and
c>0
, the condition concentration
inequality

(E.18)

\displaystyle\log\frac{1}{2}P\left\{|U(\mathsf{R}_{\hat{g},k},D)|\geq t\sqrt{v_{g^{\star},k}\left(D\right)},|\hat{g}/g^{\star}-1|\leq c\mid D\right\}

(E.19)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\leq-\frac{\delta v_{1,k}(D)}{2^{4}(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{2}\Gamma^{(1)}_{k,\varphi,b}}\frac{t^{2}}{c}\

holds with probability greater than
1-\delta
as
D
varies.

(ii)
If
g^{\star}c\geq 2
and
1/2>c>0
, then
the conditional concentration inequality

(E.20)

\displaystyle\log\frac{1}{8}P\left\{|\hat{g}-g^{\star}|>cg^{\star}\mid D\right\}\lesssim-\frac{\delta(v_{1,k}(D))^{3}}{(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{4}}\frac{z^{2}_{1-\beta/2}}{\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)}}\frac{c^{2}}{\xi^{2}}

holds with probability greater than
1-\delta
as
D
varies.

Observe that

\displaystyle P\left\{|U(\mathsf{R}_{\hat{g},k},D)-U(\mathsf{R}_{\hat{g}^{\prime},k}^{\prime},D)|\geq\lambda\sqrt{2v_{g^{\star},k}\left(D\right)}\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim P\left\{|U(\mathsf{R}_{\hat{g},k},D)|\geq\lambda\sqrt{2v_{g^{\star},k}\left(D\right)}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq P\left\{|U(\mathsf{R}_{\hat{g},k},D)|\geq\lambda\sqrt{2v_{g^{\star},k}\left(D\right)},|\hat{g}/g^{\star}-1|\leq c\mid D\right\}+P\left\{|\hat{g}/g^{\star}-1|\geq c\mid D\right\}

(E.21)

\displaystyle\lesssim\exp\left(-\frac{\delta v_{1,k(D)}}{2^{4}(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{2}\Gamma^{(1)}_{k,\varphi,b}}\frac{\lambda^{2}}{c}\right)+\exp\left(-C\frac{\delta(v_{1,k}(D))^{3}}{(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{4}}\frac{z^{2}_{1-\beta/2}}{\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)}}\frac{c^{2}}{\xi^{2}}\right)

for some universal constant
C
. Hence, it remains to choose
c
and
\lambda
such that the
quantity (
E.21
) is less than
\rho_{k,\varphi,b}\left(\xi,\beta\mid D\right)
.
First, we choose
c
such that

\displaystyle\frac{\delta(v_{1,k}(D))^{3}}{(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{4}}\frac{z^{2}_{1-\beta/2}}{\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)}}\frac{c^{2}}{\xi^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\log\left(\rho_{k,\varphi,b}\left(\xi,\beta\mid D\right)^{-1}\right)

Choosing
c
by

(E.22)

c=\xi\left(\frac{\left(2-\varphi k-\varphi\right)^{2}(\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)})^{1/2}}{z_{1-\beta/2}\delta^{1/2}(v_{1,k}(D))^{3/2}}\right)\log^{1/2}\left(\rho_{k,\varphi,b}\left(\xi,\beta\mid D\right)^{-1}\right)

suffices. Next, we choose
\lambda
such that

\frac{\delta v_{1,k(D)}}{2^{4}(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{2}\Gamma^{(1)}_{k,\varphi,b}}\frac{\lambda^{2}}{c}\lesssim\log\left(\rho_{k,\varphi,b}\left(\xi,\beta\mid D\right)^{-1}\right).

We can rewrite this condition by plugging in our choice of
c
through

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\xi}\frac{z_{1-\beta/2}\delta^{3/2}(v_{1,k}(D))^{5/2}}{\left(2-\varphi k-\varphi\right)^{4}\Gamma^{(1)}_{k,\varphi,b}(\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)})^{1/2}}\lesssim\log^{3/2}\left(\rho_{k,\varphi,b}\left(\xi,\beta\mid D\right)^{-1}\right)

Choosing
\lambda
by

\displaystyle\lambda

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\xi}{z_{1-\beta/2}}\frac{(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{4}\Gamma^{(1)}_{k,\varphi,b}(\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)})^{1/2}}{\delta^{3/2}(v_{1,k}\left(D\right))^{5/2}}\right)^{1/2}\tilde{\lambda}_{k,\varphi,b}\left(D\right),\quad\text{where}

\displaystyle\tilde{\lambda}_{k,\varphi,b}\left(D\right)

\displaystyle=\log^{3/4}\left(\rho_{k,\varphi,b}\left(\xi,\beta\mid D\right)^{-1}\right)

will then suffice, as required.

E.7.3.
Part(iii)

Observe that

\displaystyle P\left\{|\left(1-\frac{\hat{g}}{g^{\star}}\right)\left(a(\mathsf{R}_{\hat{g},k},D)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)|\geq\lambda\sqrt{2v_{g^{\star},k}\left(D\right)},|\begin{gathered}g^{\star}-\hat{g}|\leq cg^{\star}\end{gathered}\mid D\right\}

\displaystyle=P\left\{|\left(a(\mathsf{R}_{\hat{g},k},D)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)|\geq\frac{\lambda}{c}\sqrt{2v_{g^{\star},k}\left(D\right)},|\begin{gathered}g^{\star}-\hat{g}|\leq cg^{\star}\end{gathered}\mid D\right\}

\displaystyle\leq P\left\{\max_{|g^{\star}-g|\leq cg^{\star}}|\left(a(\mathsf{R}_{g,k},D)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)|\geq\frac{\lambda}{c}\sqrt{2v_{g^{\star},k}\left(D\right)}\mid D\right\}

\displaystyle\leq P\left\{\max_{|g^{\star}-g|\leq cg^{\star}}|\left(a(\mathsf{R}_{g,k},D)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)|\geq\frac{\lambda}{c}\left(\frac{\xi}{z_{1-\beta/2}}\right)\mid D\right\}

(E.25)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{|g^{\star}-g|\leq cg^{\star}}P\left\{|\left(a(\mathsf{R}_{g,k},D)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)|\geq\frac{\lambda}{c}\left(\frac{\xi}{z_{1-\beta/2}}\right)\right\}\penalty\ .

By
Theorem

D.1
, we have that

\displaystyle P\left\{|\left(a(\mathsf{R}_{g,k},D)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)|\geq\frac{\lambda}{c}\left(\frac{\xi}{z_{1-\beta/2}}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{g\delta}{2^{4}(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{2}\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}(1)}\frac{\lambda^{2}}{c^{2}}\left(\frac{\xi}{z_{1-\beta/2}}\right)^{2}\right)

and so is (
E.25
) bounded from above by

\displaystyle 4cg^{\star}\exp\left(-\frac{g^{\star}(1-c)\delta}{4(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{2}\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}(1)}\frac{\lambda^{2}}{c^{2}}\left(\frac{\xi}{z_{1-\beta/2}}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim v_{1,k}\left(D\right)c\left(\frac{z_{1-\beta/2}}{\xi}\right)^{2}\exp\left(-\frac{\delta v_{1,k}(D)}{2^{5}(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{2}\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}(1)}\frac{\lambda^{2}}{c^{2}}\right)

if
1-c\geq 1/2
by the definition of
g^{\star}
. Thus, we have
that

\displaystyle P\left\{|Q(\mathsf{R}_{\hat{g},k},D)-Q(\mathsf{R}_{\hat{g}^{\prime},k}^{\prime},D)|\geq\lambda\sqrt{2v_{g^{\star},k}\left(D\right)}\mid D\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim P\left\{|\left(1-\frac{\hat{g}}{g^{\star}}\right)\left(a(\mathsf{R}_{\hat{g},k},D)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)|\geq\lambda\sqrt{2v_{g^{\star},k}\left(D\right)},|\begin{gathered}g^{\star}-\hat{g}|\leq cg^{\star}\end{gathered}\mid D\right\}

\displaystyle+P\left\{|\begin{gathered}g^{\star}-\hat{g}|\geq cg^{\star}\end{gathered}\mid D\right\}

(E.28)

\displaystyle\lesssim v_{1,k}\left(D\right)c\left(\frac{z_{1-\beta/2}}{\xi}\right)^{2}\exp\left(-\frac{\delta v_{1,k}(D)}{2^{5}(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{2}\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(1)}}\frac{\lambda^{2}}{c^{2}}\right)

(E.29)

\displaystyle\quad+\exp\left(-\frac{1}{C}\frac{\delta(v_{1,k}(D))^{3}}{(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{4}}\frac{z_{1-\beta/2}}{\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)}}\frac{c^{2}}{\xi^{2}}\right)

for some universal constant
C
by Lemma
E.5
,
Part (ii). If
c
is chosen to satisfy (
E.22
),
the term (
E.29
) will be bounded above by
\rho_{\varphi,k}\left(\xi,\beta\mid D\right)
for all sufficiently small
\xi
. By plugging this value of
c
and
\lambda=\lambda_{\varphi,k}\left(\xi,\beta\mid D\right)

into (
E.28
), we obtain the term

\displaystyle\left(\frac{z_{1-\beta/2}^{2}}{\xi}\right)\left(\frac{\left(2-\varphi k-\varphi\right)^{2}(\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)})^{1/2}}{z_{1-\beta/2}\delta^{1/2}(v_{1,k}(D))^{1/2}}\right)\log^{1/2}\left(\rho_{k,\varphi,b}\left(\xi,\beta\mid D\right)^{-1}\right)

(E.30)

\displaystyle\exp\left(-\frac{1}{\xi}\frac{\delta^{1/2}(v_{1,k}(D))^{3/2}}{(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{2}(\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)})^{1/2}}\tilde{\lambda}_{k,\varphi,b}^{U}\left(D\right)\log\left(\rho_{k,\varphi,b}\left(\xi,\beta\mid D\right)^{-1}\right)\right)

as required. Observe that the term (
E.30
) is bounded above by
\rho_{\varphi,k}\left(\xi,\beta\mid D\right)
, for all sufficiently small
\xi
, completing the proof.

E.8.
Proofs for Supporting Lemmas

E.8.1.
Proof of
Lemma

E.1

Let
T_{i}\left(\pi_{m,\ell}\right)=T\left(\mathsf{s}_{i}\left(\pi_{\ell,m}\right),D\right)

and define
T_{i}(\pi_{m,\ell}^{\prime})
analogously. Define

R_{m}\left(\ell\right)=\left\{i\in\left[n\right]:\pi_{m,\ell}\left(i\right)\neq\pi_{m,\ell}^{\prime}\left(i\right)\right\}

and let
\overline{R}_{m}\left(\ell\right)=|R_{m}\left(\ell\right)|

denote the number of shared indices in the permutations in
\pi_{m,\ell}

and
\pi_{m,\ell}^{\prime}
. Observe that

(E.31)

\frac{1}{k}\sum_{i=1}^{k}\left(T_{i}\left(\pi_{m,\ell}\right)-T_{i}\left(\pi_{m,\ell}^{\prime}\right)\right)=0

almost surely for all
m^{\prime}\geq m
if and only if
\overline{R}_{m}\left(\ell\right)=n
.
Let
N\left(\ell\right)
denote the values of the smallest index
m

with
\overline{R}_{m}\left(\ell\right)=n
. Observe that

(E.32)

\displaystyle\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\bigg|\mathbb{E}\left[a(\mathsf{R}_{g,k}(\bm{\pi}_{m}),D)-a(\mathsf{R}_{g,k}(\bm{\pi}_{m}^{\prime}),D)\mid\bm{\pi}_{0}=\bm{\psi},\bm{\pi}_{0}^{\prime}=\bm{\psi}^{\prime},D\right]\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{g}\frac{1}{k}\sum_{\ell=1}^{g}\sum_{i=1}^{k}\mathbb{E}\left[\bigg|\left(T_{i}\left(\pi_{m,\ell}\right)-T_{i}\left(\pi_{m,\ell}^{\prime}\right)\right)\bigg|\mid\bm{\pi}_{0}=\bm{\psi},\bm{\pi}_{0}^{\prime}=\bm{\psi}^{\prime},D\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{g}\frac{1}{k}\sum_{\ell=1}^{g}\sum_{i=1}^{k}\max_{\mathsf{s},\mathsf{s}^{\prime}\in\mathsf{S}_{n,b}}\big|T\left(\mathsf{s},D\right)-T\left(\mathsf{s}^{\prime},D\right)\big|\mathbb{E}\left[N\left(\ell\right)\right]

Hence, it suffices to bound the quantity
\mathbb{E}\left[N\left(\ell\right)\right]
.

To that end, let
N_{r}\left(\ell\right)
be the value of the smallest
index
m
with
\overline{R}_{m}\left(\ell\right)\geq r
. We proceed analogously
to standard analysis of the coupon collector’s problem (see e.g.,
Section 2.2 of
Levin and Peres, 2017
). We can evaluate

\displaystyle P\left\{\overline{R}_{m}\left(\ell\right)\geq r+1\mid\overline{R}_{m-1}\left(\ell\right)=r\right\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{g}P\left\{\pi_{\ell}\left(I_{m}\right)\neq\pi_{\ell}^{\prime}\left(I_{m}\right),\pi_{\ell}^{-1}\left(J_{m}\right)\neq\pi_{\ell}^{\prime-1}\left(J_{m}\right)\right\}=\frac{\left(n-r\right)^{2}}{gn^{2}}

and

\displaystyle P\left\{\overline{R}_{m}\left(\ell\right)<r\mid\overline{R}_{m-1}\left(\ell\right)=r\right\}

\displaystyle=0,

and thereby obtain the bound

(E.33)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[N\left(\ell\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{r=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[N_{r}\left(\ell\right)-N_{r-1}\left(\ell\right)\right]\leq\sum_{r=1}^{n}\frac{gn^{2}}{\left(n-r+1\right)^{2}}=gn^{2}\sum_{r=1}^{n}\frac{1}{r^{2}}\leq 2gn^{2}.

Hence, we find that (
E.32
) is upper bounded by

2gn^{2}\max_{\mathsf{s},\mathsf{s}^{\prime}\in\mathsf{S}_{n,b}}T\left(\mathsf{s},D\right)-T\left(\mathsf{s}^{\prime},D\right),

as required.

E.8.2.
Proof of
Lemma

E.2

Observe that

\displaystyle\frac{\text{d}}{\text{d}t}e^{tx}e^{(1-t)y}

\displaystyle=\left(x-y\right)e^{tx}e^{(1-t)y}.

Thus, we find that

(Fundamental Theorem of Calculus)

\displaystyle c\left(e^{x}-e^{y}\right)

\displaystyle=c\int_{0}^{1}\left(\frac{\text{d}}{\text{d}t}e^{tx}e^{\left(1-t\right)y}\right)\text{dt}

\displaystyle=c\left(x-y\right)\int_{0}^{1}e^{tx}e^{(1-t)y}\text{dt}

(Convexity)

\displaystyle\leq c\left(x-y\right)\int_{0}^{1}\left(te^{x}+\left(1-t\right)e^{y}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{c}{2}\left(x-y\right)\left(e^{x}+e^{y}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left(\left(s^{-1}c^{2}\left(e^{x}+e^{y}\right)\right)\left(s\left(x-y\right)^{2}\left(e^{x}+e^{y}\right)\right)\right)^{1/2}

(AM-GM)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}\left(\left(s^{-1}c^{2}\right)+s\left(x-y\right)^{2}\right)\left(e^{x}+e^{y}\right),

as required.

E.8.3.
Proof of
Lemma

E.3

To begin, consider any collection of real numbers
x_{1},\ldots,x_{2^{c}}
,
for some positive integer
c
. For any integer
s>0
, we have

(Young’s Inequality)

\displaystyle\left(x_{1}\cdots x_{2^{c}}\right)^{2s}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}\left(\left(x_{i_{1}}\cdots x_{i_{2^{c-1}}}\right)^{2s}+\left(x_{i_{2^{c-1}+1}}\cdots x_{2^{c}}\right)^{2s}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}\left(\left(x_{i_{1}}\cdots x_{i_{2^{c-1}}}\right)^{2(s+1)}+2\left(x_{1}\cdots x_{2^{c}}\right)^{2s}+\left(x_{i_{2^{c-1}+1}}\cdots x_{2^{c}}\right)^{2(s+1)}\right)

and so

(E.34)

\left(x_{1}\cdots x_{2^{c}}\right)^{2s}\leq\frac{1}{2}\left(x_{i_{1}}\cdots x_{i_{2^{c-1}}}\right)^{2(s+1)}+\frac{1}{2}\left(x_{i_{2^{c-1}+1}}\cdots x_{2^{c}}\right)^{2(s+1)}.

Consequently, we have that

(E.35)

x_{1}\cdots x_{2^{c}}\leq\frac{1}{2^{c}}\sum_{i=1}^{2^{c}}x_{i}^{2c}

through
2^{r}
applications of (
E.34
).

Now, to prove the Lemma, we may assume without loss that
h_{i}>0

for all
i
by continuity. Observe that

(E.36)

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}X_{m}\right)^{2^{c}}\right]=\sum_{i_{1}=0}^{\infty}\cdots\sum_{i_{2^{c}}=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[X_{i_{1}}\cdots X_{i_{2^{c}}}\right]

by dominated convergence. By writing

X_{1}\cdots X_{2^{c}}=\prod_{j=1}^{2^{c}}\left(\frac{\prod_{k\neq j}h_{k}}{h_{j}^{2^{c-1}}}\right)^{1/2^{c}}X_{j}

we find that

\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1}\cdots X_{2^{c}}\right]\leq\frac{1}{2^{c}}\sum_{j=1}^{2^{c}}\frac{\prod_{k\neq j}h_{k}}{h_{j}^{2^{c-1}}}h_{j}^{2^{c}}=\prod_{j=1}^{2^{c}}h_{j}

by (
E.35
). Hence, by (
E.36
),
we have that

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}X_{m}\right)^{2^{c}}\right]\leq\sum_{i_{1}=0}^{\infty}\cdots\sum_{i_{2^{c}}=0}^{\infty}\prod_{j=1}^{2^{c}}h_{i_{j}}=\left(\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}h_{j}\right)^{2^{c}},

as required.

E.8.4.
Proof of Lemma
E.4

We begin by constructing Stein representers
\tilde{V}\left(Z,Z^{\prime}\right)

and
\check{V}\left(Z,Z^{\prime}\right)
for the statistic
\tilde{v}_{g,k}\left(Z\right)

and
\check{v}_{g,k}\left(Z\right)
, respectively. We will use the same
exchangeable pair
\left(Z,Z^{\prime}\right)_{m\geq 0}
and Markov
chain
\left(Z_{m},Z_{m}^{\prime}\right)_{m\geq 1}
defined in Section

D.4
. The subsequent result follows from an
argument similar to Lemma
D.1
,
again based on an idea expressed by Lemma 4.1 of
Chatterjee, 2005
.

Lemma E.6.

(i)
Let
\bm{\psi}
and
\psi^{\prime}
be two
sets, each containing
g
elements of
\mathcal{P}_{n}
.
The inequalities

\displaystyle\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\bigg|\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\tilde{v}_{g,k}\left(Z_{m}\right)-\tilde{v}_{g,k}\left(Z_{m}^{\prime}\right)\right)\mid Z_{0}=Z,Z_{0}^{\prime}=Z^{\prime}\right]\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq n^{2}\max_{\mathsf{s},\mathsf{s}^{\prime}\in\mathsf{S}_{n,b}}\left(T(\mathsf{s},D)-T(\mathsf{s}^{\prime},D)\right)^{2}\quad\text{and}

\displaystyle\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\bigg|\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\check{v}_{g,k}\left(Z_{m}\right)-\check{v}_{g,k}\left(Z_{m}^{\prime}\right)\right)\mid Z_{0}=Z,Z_{0}^{\prime}=Z^{\prime}\right]\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq 2gn^{2}\max_{\mathsf{s}\in\mathsf{S}_{n,b}}\left(T(\mathsf{s},D)-T(\mathsf{s}^{\prime},D)\right)^{2}

hold almost surely.

(ii)
The functions

\displaystyle\tilde{V}\left(Z,Z^{\prime}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\tilde{v}_{g,k}\left(Z_{m}\right)-\tilde{v}_{g,k}\left(Z_{m}^{\prime}\right)\right)\mid Z_{0}=Z,Z_{0}^{\prime}=Z^{\prime}\right]\quad\text{and}

\displaystyle\check{V}\left(Z,Z^{\prime}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\check{v}_{g,k}\left(Z_{m}\right)-\check{v}_{g,k}\left(Z_{m}^{\prime}\right)\right)\mid Z_{0}=Z,Z_{0}^{\prime}=Z^{\prime}\right]

are finite and satisfy the equalities

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{V}\left(Z,Z^{\prime}\right)\mid Z\right]

\displaystyle=\tilde{v}_{g,k}\left(Z\right)-v_{g,k}\left(D\right)\quad\text{and}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\check{V}\left(Z,Z^{\prime}\right)\mid Z\right]

\displaystyle=\check{v}_{g,k}\left(Z\right)-v_{g,k}\left(D\right)

almost surely.

Next, we apply the concentration inequality stated in Theorem
D.2
,
due to
Chatterjee, 2005
;
Chatterjee, 2007
. That
is, to establish part (i) of the Lemma, it will suffice to characterize
constants
s
and
u
that satisfy

U_{\tilde{v}}\left(Z\right)\leq s^{-1}u\quad\text{and}\quad U_{\tilde{V}}\left(Z\right)\leq su,

with probability
1-\delta
, where

U_{\tilde{v}}\left(Z\right)=\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\tilde{v}\left(Z\right)-\tilde{v}\left(Z^{\prime}\right)\right)\mid Z\right]\quad\text{and}\quad U_{\tilde{V}}\left(Z\right)=\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{V}\left(Z,Z^{\prime}\right)^{2}\mid Z\right].

The same statement holds for part (ii) of the Lemma, for the objects

U_{\check{v}}\left(Z\right)
and
U_{\check{V}}\left(Z\right)

defined analogously.

We obtain such a characterization through the application of Lemma

D.3
. To this end, observe that, by Lemma
E.6
,
part (i), the bound

(E.37)

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\tilde{v}_{g,k}\left(Z_{m}\right)-\tilde{v}_{g,k}\left(Z_{m}^{\prime}\right)\right)\mid Z_{0}=Z,Z_{0}^{\prime}=Z^{\prime}\right]\right)^{2}

(E.38)

\displaystyle\leq n^{4}\max_{\mathsf{s},\mathsf{s}^{\prime}\in\mathsf{S}_{n,b}}\left(T(\mathsf{s},D)-T(\mathsf{s}^{\prime},D)\right)^{4}

holds almost surely. The right hand side of (
E.38
)
is square integrable, as the eighth-order split-stability
\zeta^{(8)}

is finite almost surely. An analogous statement holds for the statistic

\check{v}_{g,k}\left(Z\right)
. Thus, deterministic bounds of the form
(
D.29
) can be obtained through the application
of the following Lemma.

Lemma E.7.

Suppose that
4.1
and
4.2
hold
and that the data
D
are independent and identically distributed. If the eighth-order split stability
\zeta^{(8)}
is finite,
then

(i)
The inequality

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{v}_{g,k}(Z_{m})-\tilde{v}_{g,k}(Z_{m}^{\prime})\mid Z,Z^{\prime}\right]^{2}\mid\bm{\pi}\right]\lesssim\left(1-\frac{2}{gn^{2}}\right)^{2m}\left(\frac{\left(2-\varphi k-\varphi\right)^{4}}{n^{2}g^{4}}\right)\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)}\penalty\ ,

holds almost surely for all integers
m\geq 0
, and

(ii)
The inequality

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[\check{v}_{g,k}(Z_{m})-\check{v}_{g,k}(Z_{m}^{\prime})\mid Z,Z^{\prime}\right]^{2}\mid\bm{\pi}\right]\lesssim\left(1-\frac{2}{gn^{2}}\right)^{2m}\left(\frac{\left(2-\varphi k-\varphi\right)^{4}}{n^{2}g^{3}}\right)\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)}\penalty\ ,

holds almost surely for all integers
m\geq 0
.

Thus, by Lemma
D.3
, part (i), the inequalities

\displaystyle U_{\tilde{V}}\left(Z\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\delta}\left(\frac{gn^{2}}{2}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\left(2-\varphi k-\varphi\right)^{4}}{n^{2}g^{4}}\right)\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\frac{gn^{2}}{2}\right)\left(\frac{1}{\delta}\frac{(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{4}}{g^{3}}\right)\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)}

and

\displaystyle U_{\tilde{v}}\left(Z\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\delta}\left(\frac{\left(2-\varphi k-\varphi\right)^{4}}{n^{2}g^{4}}\right)\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\frac{2}{gn^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{1}{\delta}\frac{\left(2-\varphi k-\varphi\right)^{4}}{g^{3}}\right)\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)}

both hold with probability greater than
1-\delta
. Hence, we obtain
the bound (
E.16
) by applying Theorem

D.2
and choosing
s=gn^{2}/2
and

u=\frac{1}{\delta}\frac{\left(2-\varphi k-\varphi\right)^{4}}{g^{3}}\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)}\penalty\ .

Analogous inequalities for the objects
U_{\check{v}}\left(Z\right)

and
U_{\check{V}}\left(Z\right)
hold by Lemma
D.3
,
part (ii), where in that case

u=\frac{1}{\delta}\frac{\left(2-\varphi k-\varphi\right)^{4}}{g^{2}}n^{4}\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)}\penalty\ .

which similarly implies the bound (
E.17
) by Theorem
D.2
.

E.8.5.
Proof of Lemma
E.6

Reinstate the notation of the proof of Lemma
E.1
. We begin by noting that

\displaystyle\max_{\mathsf{s},\mathsf{s}^{\prime}\in\mathsf{S}_{n,b}}\left(T(\mathsf{s},D)-T(\mathsf{s}^{\prime},D)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\max_{\mathsf{s},\mathsf{s}^{\prime}\in\mathsf{S}_{n,b}}\left(T(\mathsf{s},D)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)^{2}+\left(T(\mathsf{s}^{\prime},D)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+2\left(T(\mathsf{s},D)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)\left(T(\mathsf{s}^{\prime},D)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)

\displaystyle\geq 4\max_{\mathsf{s}\in\mathsf{S}_{n,b}}\left(T(\mathsf{s},D)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)^{2}.

Observe that

\displaystyle\sum_{i,i^{\prime}=1}^{k}\left(T_{i}\left(\pi_{m,\ell}\right)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)\left(T_{i}\left(\pi_{m,\ell}\right)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)-\sum_{i,i^{\prime}=1}^{k}\left(T_{i}\left(\pi_{m,\ell}^{\prime}\right)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)\left(T_{i^{\prime}}\left(\pi_{m,\ell}^{\prime}\right)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)=0

for all for all
m^{\prime}\geq m
if and only if
\overline{R}_{m}\left(\ell\right)=n
.
Thus, we have that

\displaystyle\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\bigg|\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{v}_{g,k}(Z_{m})-\tilde{v}_{g,k}(Z_{m}^{\prime})\mid Z_{0}=\left(\mathsf{r}\left(\bm{\psi}\right),D\right),Z_{0}^{\prime}=\left(\mathsf{r}\left(\bm{\psi}^{\prime}\right),D\right)\right]\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{g^{2}k^{2}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{g}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}=1}^{k}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\bigg|\left(T_{i}\left(\pi_{m,\ell}\right)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)\left(T_{i^{\prime}}\left(\pi_{m,\ell}\right)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad-\left(T_{i}\left(\pi_{m,\ell}^{\prime}\right)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)\left(T_{i^{\prime}}\left(\pi_{m,\ell}^{\prime}\right)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)\bigg|\mid Z_{0}=\left(\mathsf{r}\left(\bm{\psi}\right),D\right),Z_{0}^{\prime}=\left(\mathsf{r}\left(\bm{\psi}^{\prime}\right),D\right)\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{g^{2}k^{2}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{g}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}=1}^{k}\max_{\mathsf{s},\mathsf{s}^{\prime}\in\mathsf{S}_{n,b}}\big|\left(T\left(\mathsf{s},D\right)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)\left(T\left(\mathsf{s}^{\prime},D\right)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)\big|\mathbb{E}\left[N\left(\ell\right)\right].

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{g}\max_{\mathsf{s}\in\mathsf{S}_{n,b}}\left(T(\mathsf{s},D)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[N\left(\ell\right)\right].

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2g}\max_{\mathsf{s},\mathsf{s}^{\prime}\in\mathsf{S}_{n,b}}\left(T(\mathsf{s},D)-T(\mathsf{s}^{\prime},D)\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[N\left(\ell\right)\right].

Similarly, we have that

\displaystyle\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\bigg|\mathbb{E}\left[\check{v}_{g,k}(Z_{m})-\check{v}_{g,k}(Z_{m}^{\prime})\mid Z_{0}=\left(\mathsf{r}\left(\bm{\psi}\right),D\right),Z_{0}^{\prime}=\left(\mathsf{r}\left(\bm{\psi}^{\prime}\right),D\right)\right]\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{g^{2}k^{2}}\sum_{\ell,\ell^{\prime}=1}^{g}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}=1}^{k}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\bigg|\left(T_{i}\left(\pi_{m,\ell}\right)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)\left(T_{i^{\prime}}\left(\pi_{m,\ell^{\prime}}\right)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad-\left(T_{i}\left(\pi_{m,\ell}^{\prime}\right)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)\left(T_{i^{\prime}}\left(\pi_{m,\ell^{\prime}}^{\prime}\right)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)\bigg|\mid Z_{0}=\left(\mathsf{r}\left(\bm{\psi}\right),D\right),Z_{0}^{\prime}=\left(\mathsf{r}\left(\bm{\psi}^{\prime}\right),D\right)\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{g^{2}k^{2}}\sum_{\ell,\ell^{\prime}=1}^{g}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}=1}^{k}\max_{\mathsf{s},\mathsf{s}^{\prime}\in\mathsf{S}_{n,b}}\big|\left(T\left(\mathsf{s},D\right)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)\left(T\left(\mathsf{s}^{\prime},D\right)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)\big|\mathbb{E}\left[\max\left\{N\left(\ell\right),N\left(\ell^{\prime}\right)\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{g^{2}k^{2}}\sum_{\ell,\ell^{\prime}=1}^{g}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}=1}^{k}\max_{\mathsf{s},\mathsf{s}^{\prime}\in\mathsf{S}_{n,b}}\big|\left(T\left(\mathsf{s},D\right)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)\left(T\left(\mathsf{s}^{\prime},D\right)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)\big|\mathbb{E}\left[N\left(\ell\right)+N\left(\ell^{\prime}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\max_{\mathsf{s},\mathsf{s}^{\prime}\in\mathsf{S}_{n,b}}\left(T(\mathsf{s},D)-T(\mathsf{s}^{\prime},D)\right)^{2}\big|\mathbb{E}\left[N\left(\ell\right)+N\left(\ell^{\prime}\right)\right]

Thus, part (i) of the Lemma follows by the bound (
E.33
).
Part (ii) of the Lemma then follows by an argument analogous to the
proof of Lemma
D.1
.

E.8.6.
Proof of Lemma
E.7

We reinstate the notation introduced in the proof of Lemma
D.4

from Section
E.4
. First, observe
that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{v}_{g,k}(Z_{m})-\tilde{v}_{g,k}(Z_{m}^{\prime})\mid Z,Z^{\prime}\right]^{2}\mid\bm{\pi}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(P\left\{\mathcal{H}_{m}\right\}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{v}_{g,k}(Z_{m})-\tilde{v}_{g,k}(Z_{m}^{\prime})\mid\mathcal{H}_{m},Z,Z^{\prime}\right]\right)^{2}\mid\bm{\pi}\right]

(E.39)

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{2}{gn^{2}}\right)^{2m}\left(\frac{2kb\left(2n-bk-b\right)}{n^{2}}\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\tilde{v}_{g,k}(Z_{m})-\tilde{v}_{g,k}(Z_{m}^{\prime})\right)^{2}\mid\mathcal{H}_{m},\bm{\pi}\right]

as before. Recall the notation

\overline{a}(\mathsf{r}_{\ell},D)=\frac{1}{k}\sum_{i=1}^{k}(T(\mathsf{s}_{\ell,i},D)-\overline{a}(D))

and observe that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\tilde{v}_{g,k}(Z_{m})-\tilde{v}_{g,k}(Z_{m}^{\prime})\right)^{2}\mid\mathcal{H}_{m},\bm{\pi}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{g^{4}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\overline{a}(\mathsf{r}(\pi_{m,L}),D)^{2}-\overline{a}(\mathsf{r}(\pi^{\prime}_{m,L}),D)^{2}\right)^{2}\mid\mathcal{H}_{m}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{g^{4}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\overline{a}(\mathsf{r}(\pi_{m,L}),D)+\overline{a}(\mathsf{r}(\pi^{\prime}_{m,L}),D)\right)^{2}\left(\overline{a}(\mathsf{r}(\pi_{m,L}),D)-\overline{a}(\mathsf{r}(\pi^{\prime}_{m,L}),D)\right)^{2}\mid\mathcal{H}_{m}\right]

(Cauchy-Schwarz)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{g^{4}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\overline{a}(\mathsf{r}(\pi_{m,L}),D)+\overline{a}(\mathsf{r}(\pi^{\prime}_{m,L}),D)\right)^{4}\mid\mathcal{H}_{m}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\cdot\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\overline{a}(\mathsf{r}(\pi_{m,L}),D)-\overline{a}(\mathsf{r}(\pi^{\prime}_{m,L}),D)\right)^{4}\mid\mathcal{H}_{m}\right]\right)^{1/2}

(Hölder)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2^{4}}{g^{4}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\overline{a}(\mathsf{r}(\pi_{m,L}),D)-\overline{a}(D)\right)^{4}\right]\right)^{1/2}

(E.40)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\cdot\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\overline{a}(\mathsf{r}(\pi_{m,L}),D)-\overline{a}(\mathsf{r}(\pi^{\prime}_{m,L}),D)\right)^{4}\mid\mathcal{H}_{m}\right]\right)^{1/2}\penalty\ .

Observe that

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\overline{a}(\mathsf{r}(\pi_{m,L}),D)-\overline{a}(D)\right)^{4}\right]\leq 3^{2}\left(2^{4}(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{2}\right)^{2}\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)}

by
Theorem

D.2
, as Assumption
4.1
is maintained and the eighth-order sample-split stability
\zeta^{(8)}
is finite. In turn, we have that

(E.41)

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\overline{a}(\mathsf{r}(\pi_{m,L}),D)-\overline{a}(\mathsf{r}(\pi^{\prime}_{m,L}),D)\right)^{4}\mid\mathcal{H}_{m}\right]\leq\frac{4}{k^{4}b^{4}}\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)}

by (
E.7
), (
E.13
), and (
E.14
). Hence, we have that

(E.42)

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\tilde{v}_{g,k}(Z_{m})-\tilde{v}_{g,k}(Z_{m}^{\prime})\right)^{2}\mid\mathcal{H}_{m},\bm{\pi}\right]\leq\frac{3\cdot 2^{9}}{k^{2}b^{2}g^{4}}(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{2}\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)}\penalty\ .

Combining (
E.39
), (
E.41
), (
E.42
), we find that

\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{v}_{g,k}(Z_{m})-\tilde{v}_{g,k}(Z_{m}^{\prime})\mid Z,Z^{\prime}\right]^{2}\mid\bm{\pi}\right]\leq\left(1-\frac{2}{gn^{2}}\right)^{2m}\left(\frac{\left(3\cdot 2^{11}\right)\left(2-\varphi k-\varphi\right)^{4}}{n^{2}g^{4}}\right)\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)}\penalty\ ,

which completes the proof of the first part of the Lemma.

Second, following the same argument, we again have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[\check{v}_{g,k}(Z_{m})-\check{v}_{g,k}(Z_{m}^{\prime})\mid Z,Z^{\prime}\right]^{2}\mid\bm{\pi}\right]

(E.43)

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{2}{gn^{2}}\right)^{2m}\left(\frac{2kb\left(2n-bk-b\right)}{n^{2}}\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\check{v}_{g,k}(Z_{m})-\check{v}_{g,k}(Z_{m}^{\prime})\right)^{2}\mid\mathcal{H}_{m},\bm{\pi}\right]

In this case, we can compute

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\check{v}_{g,k}(Z_{m})-\check{v}_{g,k}(Z_{m}^{\prime})\right)^{2}\mid\mathcal{H}_{m},\bm{\pi}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left(a(Z_{m})-\overline{a}(D)\right)^{2}-\left(a(Z^{\prime}_{m})-\overline{a}(D)\right)^{2}\right)^{2}\mid\mathcal{H}_{m}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(a(Z_{m})-\overline{a}(D)+a(Z^{\prime}_{m})-\overline{a}(D)\right)^{2}\left(a(Z_{m})-a(Z^{\prime}_{m})\right)^{2}\mid\mathcal{H}_{m}\right]

(Cauchy-Schwarz)

\displaystyle\leq\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left(a(Z_{m})-\overline{a}(D)+a(Z^{\prime}_{m})-\overline{a}(D)\right)^{4}\mid\mathcal{H}_{m}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\cdot\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left(a(Z_{m})-a(Z^{\prime}_{m})\right)^{4}\mid\mathcal{H}_{m}\right]\right)^{1/2}

(E.44)

\displaystyle\leq 2^{4}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left(a(Z_{m})-\overline{a}(D)\right)^{4}\right]\right)^{1/2}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left(a(Z_{m})-a(Z^{\prime}_{m})\right)^{4}\mid\mathcal{H}_{m}\right]\right)^{1/2}\penalty\ ,

where the last inequality follows from Hölder’s inequality. Again we have that

(E.45)

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(a(Z_{m})-a(Z^{\prime}_{m})\right)^{4}\right]\leq 3^{2}\left(\frac{2^{4}\varphi^{2}(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{2}}{g}\right)^{2}\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)}

by
Theorem

D.2
, as Assumption
4.1
is maintained and the eighth-order sample-split stability
\zeta^{(8)}
is finite. Similarly. we have that

(E.46)

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(a(Z_{m})-\overline{a}(D)\right)^{4}\mid\mathcal{H}_{m}\right]\leq\frac{4}{g^{4}k^{4}b^{4}}\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)}

by (
E.7
), (
E.13
), and (
E.14
). Combining (
E.43
), (
E.45
), (
E.46
), we find that

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\check{v}_{g,k}(Z_{m})-\check{v}_{g,k}(Z_{m}^{\prime})\right)^{2}\mid\mathcal{H}_{m},\bm{\pi}\right]\leq\left(1-\frac{2}{gn^{2}}\right)^{2m}\left(\frac{\left(3\cdot 2^{9}\right)\varphi^{4}\left(2-\varphi k-\varphi\right)^{4}}{n^{2}g^{3}}\right)\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)}\penalty\ ,

which completes the proof of the second part of the Lemma.

E.8.7.
Proof of Lemma
E.5
, Part (i)

Observe that

\displaystyle P\left\{|U(\mathsf{R}_{\hat{g},k},D)|\geq t\sqrt{v_{g^{\star},k}\left(D\right)},|\hat{g}-g^{\star}|\leq cg^{\star}\mid D\right\}

\displaystyle=P\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{g}\overline{a}\left(\mathsf{r}_{i,k},D\right)-\sum_{i=1}^{g^{\star}}\overline{a}\left(\mathsf{r}_{i,k},D\right)\geq tg^{\star}\sqrt{v_{g^{\star},k}\left(D\right)},|\hat{g}-g^{\star}|\leq cg^{\star}\mid D\right\}

\displaystyle\leq P\left\{\max_{g^{\star}(1-c)\leq g\leq g^{\star}}|\sum_{i=1}^{g}\overline{a}\left(\mathsf{r}_{i,k},D\right)-\sum_{i=1}^{g^{\star}}\overline{a}\left(\mathsf{r}_{i,k},D\right)|\geq tg^{\star}\sqrt{v_{g^{\star},k}\left(D\right)}\mid D\right\}

\displaystyle+P\left\{\max_{g^{\star}\leq g\leq g^{\star}(1+c)}|\sum_{i=1}^{g}\overline{a}\left(\mathsf{r}_{i,k},D\right)-\sum_{i=1}^{g^{\star}}\overline{a}\left(\mathsf{r}_{i,k},D\right)|\geq tg^{\star}\sqrt{v_{g^{\star},k}\left(D\right)}\mid D\right\}

\displaystyle=P\left\{\max_{g^{\star}(1-c)\leq g\leq g^{\star}}|\sum_{i=1}^{g^{\star}-g}\overline{a}\left(\mathsf{r}_{i,k},D\right)|\geq tg^{\star}\sqrt{v_{g^{\star},k}\left(D\right)}\mid D\right\}

(E.47)

\displaystyle+P\left\{\max_{g^{\star}\leq g\leq g^{\star}(1+c)}|\sum_{i=1}^{g-g^{\star}}\overline{a}\left(\mathsf{r}_{i,k},D\right)|\geq tg^{\star}\sqrt{v_{g^{\star},k}\left(D\right)}\mid D\right\}.

To bound the two probabilities (
E.47
),
we apply the following Chernoff-type variation to the Kolmogorov maximal
inequality, due to
Steiger, 1970
.

Theorem E.2
(
Steiger, 1970
)
.

Let
S_{i}
,
i=1,2,\ldots
, be a real-valued
martingale sequence. If the moment generating function

m_{n}\left(\theta\right)=\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\theta S_{n}\right)\right]

is finite for all positive
\theta
, then the inequality

\log\text{ }P\left\{\max_{1\leq n^{\prime}\leq n}S_{n^{\prime}}>t\right\}\leq\inf_{\theta>0}\left(\log m_{n}\left(\theta\right)-\theta t\right),

holds.

Conditional on
D
, the random variables

\overline{a}\left(\mathsf{r}_{i,k},D\right),\quad i=1,2,\ldots,

are mean-zero, independent, and identically distributed. Thus, the
partial sums

S_{m}=\sum_{i=1}^{m}\overline{a}\left(\mathsf{r}_{i,k},D\right)

are a martingale sequence. Moreover, though inspection of the proof
of
Lemma

D.2
, we find that Theorem

D.1
implies that

\displaystyle\inf_{\theta>0}\left(\log\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\theta S_{\lfloor cg^{\star}\rfloor}\right)\mid D\right]-\theta\tau\right)

\displaystyle=\inf_{\theta>0}\left(\log\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\frac{\theta}{\lfloor cg^{\star}\rfloor}S_{\lfloor cg^{\star}\rfloor}\right)\mid D\right]-\theta\frac{\tau}{\lfloor cg^{\star}\rfloor}\right)

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{\lfloor cg^{\star}\rfloor}{2^{4}(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{2}\Gamma^{(1)}_{k,\varphi,b}}\frac{\delta\tau^{2}}{\left(\lfloor cg^{\star}\rfloor\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{\delta}{2^{4}(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{2}\Gamma^{(1)}_{k,\varphi,b}}\frac{\tau^{2}}{cg^{\star}}

with probability greater than
1-\delta
, as
4.1

holds and the fourth-order split stability
\zeta^{(4)}
is finite.
Thus, by setting

\tau=tg^{\star}\sqrt{v_{g^{\star},k}\left(D\right)}=\sqrt{g^{\star}}t\left(v_{1,k}(D)\right)^{1/2}

we find that

\displaystyle\log\frac{1}{2}P\left\{|U(\mathsf{R}_{\hat{g},k},D)|\geq t\sqrt{v_{g^{\star},k}\left(D\right)},|\hat{g}-g^{\star}|\leq cg^{\star}\mid D\right\}

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{\delta v_{1,k}(D)}{2^{4}(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{2}\Gamma^{(1)}_{k,\varphi,b}}\frac{t^{2}}{c}

with probability greater than
1-\delta
, by
Theorem

E.2

and the inequality (
E.47
).

E.8.8.
Proof of
Lemma

E.5
, Part (ii)

Observe that

(E.48)

P\left\{|\hat{g}-g^{\star}|>cg^{\star}\mid D\right\}=P\left\{g^{\star}\left(1+c\right)<\hat{g}\mid D\right\}+P\left\{\hat{g}<g^{\star}\left(1-c\right)\mid D\right\}.

We begin by handling the first term. We have that

\displaystyle P\left\{g^{\star}\left(1+c\right)<\hat{g}\mid D\right\}

\displaystyle\leq P\left\{\hat{v}_{g^{\star}\left(1+c\right),k}\left(Z\right)-v_{g^{\star}\left(1+c\right),k}\left(D\right)+v_{g^{\star}\left(1+c\right),k}\left(D\right)>\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{\xi}{z_{1-\beta/2}}\right)^{2}\mid D\right\}

\displaystyle\leq P\left\{\hat{v}_{g^{\star}\left(1+c\right),k}\left(Z\right)-v_{g^{\star}\left(1+c\right),k}\left(D\right)>\frac{v_{1,k}(D)}{g^{\star}}-\frac{v_{1,k}(D)}{g^{\star}\left(1+c\right)}\mid D\right\}

\displaystyle=P\left\{\hat{v}_{g^{\star}\left(1+c\right),k}\left(Z\right)-v_{g^{\star}\left(1+c\right),k}\left(D\right)>\frac{v_{1,k}(D)}{g^{\star}}\frac{c}{1+c}\mid D\right\},

where the first inequality follows the definition of
g^{\star}
.
Thus, as
4.1
holds and the eighth-order
split stability
\zeta^{(8)}
is finite, we have that

\displaystyle\log\frac{1}{4}P\left\{g^{\star}\left(1+c\right)<\hat{g}\mid D\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim-\frac{\delta(v_{1,k}(D))^{2}}{(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{4}}\frac{g^{\star}(1+c)c^{2}}{\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim-\frac{\delta(v_{1,k}(D))^{3}}{(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{4}}\frac{z^{2}_{1-\beta/2}c^{2}}{\xi^{2}\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)}}

with probability greater than
1-\delta
, by
Lemma

D.5
,
the definition of
g^{\star}
and the assumption that
0<c<1/2
.

Next, we bound the second term in (
E.48
). Define
the partial sums

\displaystyle A_{g}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\ell=1}^{g}\left(\frac{1}{k^{2}}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}=1}^{k}\left(T(\mathsf{s}_{\ell,i},D)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)\left(T(\mathsf{s}_{\ell,i^{\prime}},D)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)-v_{1,k}(D)\right)\quad\text{and}

\displaystyle B_{g}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\ell=1}^{g}\Bigg(\frac{1}{k^{2}}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}=1}^{k}\left(T(\mathsf{s}_{\ell,i},D)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)\left(T(\mathsf{s}_{\ell,i^{\prime}},D)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+2\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=1}^{\ell-1}\left(T(\mathsf{s}_{\ell,i},D)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)\left(T(\mathsf{s}_{\ell^{\prime},i^{\prime}},D)-\overline{a}\left(D\right)\right)-v_{1,k}(D)\Bigg).

Observe that the equality

\hat{v}_{g,k}\left(Z\right)-v_{g,k}\left(D\right)=\frac{g}{g-1}\left(\tilde{v}_{g,k}\left(Z\right)-v_{g,k}\left(D\right)\right)-\frac{1}{g-1}\left(\check{v}_{g,k}\left(Z\right)-v_{g,k}\left(D\right)\right),

from the proof of
Lemma

D.5
,
implies that

\displaystyle\hat{v}_{g,k}\left(Z\right)-v_{g,k}\left(D\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{g\left(g-1\right)}\sum_{\ell=1}^{g}\frac{1}{k^{2}}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}=1}^{k}\left(T(\mathsf{s}_{\ell,i},D)-a\left(Z\right)\right)\left(T(\mathsf{s}_{\ell,i^{\prime}},D)-a\left(Z\right)\right)-\frac{v_{1,k}(D)}{g}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{g-1}\frac{1}{g}A_{g}-\frac{1}{g-1}\frac{1}{g^{2}}B_{g}

for all positive
g
. Thus, we can write

\displaystyle P\left\{\hat{g}<g^{\star}\left(1-c\right)\mid D\right\}

\displaystyle=P\left\{\min_{2\leq g^{\prime}\leq g^{\star}\left(1-c\right)}\hat{v}_{g^{\prime},k}\left(Z\right)\leq\left(\frac{\xi}{z_{1-\beta/2}}\right)^{2}\mid D\right\}

\displaystyle=P\left\{\min_{2\leq g^{\prime}\leq g^{\star}\left(1-c\right)}\frac{\left(g^{\prime}A_{g^{\prime}}-B_{g^{\prime}}\right)}{\left(g^{\prime}-1\right)\left(g^{\prime}\right)^{2}}+\frac{1}{g^{\prime}}v_{1,k}(D)\leq\left(\frac{\xi}{z_{1-\beta/2}}\right)^{2}\mid D\right\},

where the first equality follows from the definition of
\hat{g}
.
Now, in the event that

(E.49)

\min_{2\leq g^{\prime}\leq g^{\star}\left(1-c\right)}\frac{\left(g^{\prime}A_{g^{\prime}}-B_{g^{\prime}}\right)}{\left(g^{\prime}-1\right)\left(g^{\prime}\right)^{2}}+\frac{1}{g^{\prime}}v_{1,k}(D)\leq\left(\frac{\xi}{z_{1-\beta/2}}\right)^{2},

it must also be the case that

(E.50)

\displaystyle\min_{2\leq g^{\prime}\leq g^{\star}\left(1-c\right)}\left(g^{\prime}-1\right)\left(g^{\prime}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\left(g^{\prime}A_{g^{\prime}}-B_{g^{\prime}}\right)}{\left(g^{\prime}-1\right)\left(g^{\prime}\right)^{2}}+\frac{1}{g^{\prime}}v_{1,k}(D)\right)\leq

\displaystyle(\hat{g}-1)(\hat{g})^{2}\left(\frac{\xi}{z_{1-\beta/2}}\right)^{2}

as
\hat{g}\leq g^{\star}\left(1-c\right)
necessarily. But then,
similarly, (
E.50
) implies that

\displaystyle\min_{2\leq g^{\prime}\leq g^{\star}\left(1-c\right)}\left(g^{\prime}A_{g^{\prime}}-B_{g^{\prime}}\right)+\frac{(\hat{g}-1)(\hat{g})^{2}}{g^{\prime}}v_{1,k}(D)

\displaystyle\leq(\hat{g}-1)(\hat{g})^{2}\left(\frac{\xi}{z_{1-\beta/2}}\right)^{2},

and in turn

\displaystyle\min_{2\leq g^{\prime}\leq g^{\star}\left(1-c\right)}\frac{\left(g^{\prime}A_{g^{\prime}}-B_{g^{\prime}}\right)}{\left(g^{\star}\left(1-c\right)-1\right)\left(g^{\star}\left(1-c\right)\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(\hat{g}-1)(\hat{g})^{2}}{\left(g^{\star}\left(1-c\right)-1\right)\left(g^{\star}\left(1-c\right)\right)^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{g^{\star}-1}-\frac{1}{g^{\star}\left(1-c\right)}\right)v_{1,k}(D)

\displaystyle=\frac{(\hat{g}-1)(\hat{g})^{2}}{\left(g^{\star}\left(1-c\right)-1\right)\left(g^{\star}\left(1-c\right)\right)^{2}}\left(\frac{1-g^{\star}c}{g^{\star}\left(g^{\star}-1\right)\left(1-c\right)}\right)v_{1,k}(D),

are then also true. Finally, again as (
E.49
)
is equivalent to
\hat{g}<g^{\star}\left(1-c\right)
, we have that

\displaystyle P\left\{\min_{2\leq g^{\prime}\leq g^{\star}\left(1-c\right)}\frac{\left(g^{\prime}A_{g^{\prime}}-B_{g^{\prime}}\right)}{\left(g^{\prime}-1\right)\left(g^{\prime}\right)^{2}}+\frac{1}{g^{\prime}}v_{1,k}(D)\leq\left(\frac{\xi}{z_{1-\beta/2}}\right)^{2}\mid D\right\}

\displaystyle\leq P\left\{\min_{2\leq g^{\prime}\leq g^{\star}\left(1-c\right)}\frac{g^{\prime}A_{g^{\prime}}-B_{g^{\prime}}}{\left(g^{\star}\left(1-c\right)-1\right)\left(g^{\star}\left(1-c\right)\right)^{2}}\leq\left(\frac{1-g^{\star}c}{g^{\star}\left(g^{\star}-1\right)\left(1-c\right)}\right)v_{1,k}(D)\mid D\right\}.

Now, observe that we can write

\displaystyle P\left\{\min_{2\leq g^{\prime}\leq g^{\star}\left(1-c\right)}\frac{g^{\prime}A_{g^{\prime}}-B_{g^{\prime}}}{\left(g^{\star}\left(1-c\right)-1\right)\left(g^{\star}\left(1-c\right)\right)^{2}}\leq\left(\frac{1-g^{\star}c}{g^{\star}\left(g^{\star}-1\right)\left(1-c\right)}\right)v_{1,k}(D)\mid D\right\}

\displaystyle=P\left\{\max_{2\leq g^{\prime}\leq g^{\star}\left(1-c\right)}\frac{B_{g^{\prime}}-g^{\prime}A_{g^{\prime}}}{\left(g^{\star}\left(1-c\right)-1\right)\left(g^{\star}\left(1-c\right)\right)^{2}}\geq\left(\frac{g^{\star}c-1}{g^{\star}\left(g^{\star}-1\right)\left(1-c\right)}\right)v_{1,k}(D)\mid D\right\}\penalty\ .

We bound this term by combining the argument used
to establish
Lemma

D.5

with an application of
Theorem

E.2
. To this end, observe
that

\displaystyle P\left\{\max_{2\leq g^{\prime}\leq g}\frac{B_{g^{\prime}}-g^{\prime}A_{g^{\prime}}}{\left(g-1\right)g^{2}}\leq t\mid D\right\}

\displaystyle\geq P\left\{\max_{2\leq g^{\prime}\leq g}\frac{-1}{g-1}\frac{g^{\prime}}{g^{2}}A_{g^{\prime}}\leq\frac{1}{1+\sqrt{g}}t\mid D\right\}+P\left\{\max_{2\leq g^{\prime}\leq g}\frac{1}{g-1}\frac{1}{g^{2}}B_{g^{\prime}}\leq\frac{\sqrt{g}}{1+\sqrt{g}}t\mid D\right\}-1

\displaystyle\geq P\left\{\max_{2\leq g^{\prime}\leq g}\frac{g}{g-1}\frac{-1}{g^{2}}A_{g^{\prime}}\leq\frac{\sqrt{g}}{1+\sqrt{g}}t\mid D\right\}+P\left\{\max_{2\leq g^{\prime}\leq g}\frac{1}{g-1}\frac{1}{g^{2}}B_{g^{\prime}}\leq\frac{1}{1+\sqrt{g}}t\mid D\right\}-1

(E.51)

\displaystyle\geq P\left\{\max_{2\leq g^{\prime}\leq g}-A_{g^{\prime}}\leq g^{2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{g}}\frac{g-1}{1+\sqrt{g}}t\mid D\right\}+P\left\{\max_{2\leq g^{\prime}\leq g}|B_{g^{\prime}}|\leq g^{2}\frac{g-1}{1+\sqrt{g}}t\mid D\right\}-1

for any
t>0
and any positive integer
D
. Observe that the partial
sums
A_{g}
and
B_{g}
are both martingale sequences. As
4.1
holds and the eighth-order split stability

\zeta^{(8)}
is finite, though inspection of the proof of
Lemma

D.2
,
Lemma

E.4
implies that
implies that

\displaystyle\inf_{\theta>0}\left(\log\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\theta A_{g}\right)\mid D\right]-\theta g^{2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{g}}\frac{g-1}{1+\sqrt{g}}t\right)

\displaystyle=\inf_{\theta>0}\left(\log\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\frac{\theta}{g^{2}}A_{g}\right)\mid D\right]-\theta\frac{1}{\sqrt{g}}\frac{g-1}{1+\sqrt{g}}t\right)

(E.52)

\displaystyle\lesssim-\frac{\delta}{(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{4}}\frac{g^{3}t^{2}}{\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)}}

and

\displaystyle\inf_{\theta>0}\left(\log\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\theta B_{g}\right)\mid D\right]-\theta g^{2}\frac{g-1}{1+\sqrt{g}}t\right)

\displaystyle=\inf_{\theta>0}\left(\log\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\frac{\theta}{g^{2}}B_{g}\right)\mid D\right]-\theta\frac{g-1}{1+\sqrt{g}}t\right)

(E.53)

\displaystyle\lesssim-\frac{\delta}{(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{4}}\frac{g^{3}t^{2}}{\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)}}

each with probability greater than
1-\delta
, where we have used
the facts that
4g\leq\left(1+\sqrt{g}\right)^{2}
for all
g\geq 1

and
\left(1/4\right)g^{2}\geq\left(g-1\right)^{2}
for all
g\geq 2
.
Hence, by
Theorem

E.2
, and plugging (
E.52
)
and (
E.53
) into (
E.51
),
we find that

\displaystyle\log\frac{1}{4}P\left\{\max_{2\leq g^{\prime}\leq g}\frac{B_{g^{\prime}}-g^{\prime}A_{g^{\prime}}}{\left(g^{\prime}-1\right)\left(g^{\prime}\right)^{2}}\geq t\mid D\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim-\frac{\delta}{(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{4}}\frac{g^{3}t^{2}}{\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)}}

with probability greater than
1-\delta
. Consequently, by the definition of
g^{\star}
, we find that

\displaystyle\log\frac{1}{4}P\left\{g^{\star}-\hat{g}\geq c\mid D\right\}

\displaystyle=\log\frac{1}{4}P\left\{\max_{2\leq g^{\prime}\leq g^{\star}\left(1-c\right)}\frac{B_{g^{\prime}}-g^{\prime}A_{g^{\prime}}}{\left(g^{\star}\left(1-c\right)-1\right)\left(g^{\star}\left(1-c\right)\right)^{2}}\geq\left(\frac{g^{\star}c-1}{g^{\star}\left(g^{\star}-1\right)\left(1-c\right)}\right)v_{1,k}(D)\mid D\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim-\frac{\delta(v_{1,k}(D))^{2}}{(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{4}}\frac{g^{\star}(1-c)(g^{\star}c-1)^{2}}{(g^{\star}-1)^{2}\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim-\frac{\delta(v_{1,k}(D))^{2}}{(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{4}}\frac{g^{\star}c^{2}}{\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim-\frac{\delta(v_{1,k}(D))^{3}}{(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{4}}\frac{z^{2}_{1-\beta/2}c^{2}}{\xi^{2}\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)}}

where in the second to last inequality we have used the facts that

\frac{1}{2}x\leq x-1
for
x\geq 2
and
g^{\star}c\geq 2
. Thus,
putting the pieces together, we find that

\displaystyle\log\frac{1}{8}P\left\{|\hat{g}-g^{\star}|>cg^{\star}\mid D\right\}\lesssim-\frac{\delta(v_{1,k}(D))^{3}}{(2-\varphi k-\varphi)^{4}}\frac{z^{2}_{1-\beta/2}c^{2}}{\xi^{2}\Gamma_{k,\varphi,b}^{(2)}}

with probability greater than
1-\delta
, as required.
